| 06-12 | 11.02 FAQ — a charge made outside the Charge phase still resolves Charge-step steps 2-3 | FAQ | implemented | NO ESCAPE (Custodes, in the opponent's Movement phase) rolls 2D6, then attempts a move that must end engaged (CustodesCodexMovement.gd:523-583). Heroic Intervention also rolls first, then moves (ChargePhase.gd:3984, 4025). |
| 06-12 | 11.02.01 Commentary — an unmodified charge roll of 2 never completes a charge | Commentary | implemented | Charge targets must be within min(12, roll) (ChargeMove11e.gd:74-79, 106-107), and a unit eligible to charge is unengaged (more than 2" away), so a roll of 2 has no selectable target. |
| 06-12 | 12.08 Errata (under 11.03) — Ongoing Consolidation: each model stays engaged; unfought engaged enemies must be selected to fight | Errata | partial | Newly engaged, unfought enemies are forced to fight after every consolidation mode, Ongoing included (FightPhase.gd:3274-3301 via _scan_newly_eligible_units_after_consolidation, 4778-4916). But units that were eligible and passed are marked fought (4377-4392) and skipped by the scan (4804), so they are never forced (known issue FC-5). Objective consolidations also trigger forced fights. The 'each model' clause is checked per unit, not per model (ConsolidationMove.gd:97, 169-175). |
| 06-12 | FAQ (under 11.03) — a rule changing pile-in/consolidation distance does not change target-selection distances | FAQ | implemented | The pile-in 5" and engaging/objective 3" selection ranges are constants that no distance rule scales (PileInMove.gd:17, :52; ConsolidationMove.gd:22, :31, :88-91). |
| 06-12 | FAQ (under 11.03) — extra pile-in distance is used in the Pile-in step, extra consolidation distance in the Consolidate step | FAQ | implemented | LIGHTNING WRATH's D3+3" applies only while the Pile In step is ACTIVE; an overrun pile-in stays 3" (FightPhase.gd:6511). TACTICAL FOCUS is offered in the Consolidate step's unit_selected_to_consolidate window (FightController.gd:2974-2978). |
| 06-12 | FAQ (under 11.03) — a rule saying the unit 'can move up to X"' on a pile-in/consolidation changes that move's maximum distance | FAQ | partial | Pile-in maxima are honoured (FightPhase.gd:6511; the Custodes 4" cap via _fight_base_move_cap, 6399-6402). But _validate_consolidate_11e caps every model at a 3" base (FightPhase.gd:6610, via 6382-6402) and never reads effect_consolidate_max (TACTICAL FOCUS, ALWAYS LOOKIN' FER A FIGHT). Only the UI drag limit reads it (FightPhase.gd:2853-2880; FightController.gd:2977, 3000). |
| 06-12 | 12.04 FAQ — a unit with no melee weapons can be selected to fight (makes no attacks) | FAQ | implemented | FightSequencer eligibility has no weapon filter (FightSequencer.gd:104-133, 164-183). _validate_select_fighter needs no weapons (FightPhase.gd:708-747). An activation with no melee weapons just makes no attacks (FightController.gd:1702-1704). |
| 06-12 | 12.04.01 Commentary — eligible but unable to fight: pass when all eligible units are more than 5" from enemies | Commentary | implemented | FightSequencer.can_pass, pass_selection and peek_selection: a pass needs every eligible unit more than 5" from all enemies; it lapses when the sequence returns; the Fight step ends on two passes in a row or a pass against an opponent with nothing eligible (FightSequencer.gd:198-279). |
| 13-16 | 13.01 FAQ (within the boundary = within the terrain area) | FAQ | partial | No model has elevation (elevation_inches is never written), so a model on an upper floor counts by its footprint. Terrain objectives and Hidden use any-part overlap (MissionManager.gd:511-516; TerrainManager.gd:927-941,1035). The 13.08 in-area cover test checks only the base centre (TerrainManager.gd:1419 via area_at :847-852). |
| 13-16 | 13.02.01 Commentary (creating your own battlefield) | Commentary | no_engine_surface | Advice to players on building a board; the engine loads authored layouts (TerrainManager.gd:158-252). |
| 13-16 | 13.06.01 Commentary (Solid terrain and movement) | Commentary | partial | Non-INFANTRY models cannot end a move overlapping tall dense features (TerrainManager.gd:1283-1295). INFANTRY/BEASTS/SWARM/MOBILE are exempt (:1286-1288; MovementPhase.gd:779-788, commented 'infantry exempt'). A feature containing a model's centre stops Solid-blocking that model's sight lines (TerrainManager.gd:947-970,1187-1190). |
| 13-16 | 13.08 FAQ (benefit of cover per attacking model) | FAQ | partial | Cover is applied per attack from cover_model_per_attack (RulesEngine.gd:2766-2794, 4968-4981). Rapid Fire, Blast and +A bonus attacks are never given a firing model, so they take the first model's view (2368-2370, 2386-2388, 2410-2451; 4573-4655). |
| 13-16 | 13.09 Errata (Hidden first bullet: light/dense) | Errata | implemented | is_model_hidden requires INFANTRY/BEASTS/SWARM and any part of the base within a terrain-area group that contains a light or dense piece (TerrainManager.gd:1008-1015, 1030-1050). |
| 13-16 | 13.09.01 Commentary (Hidden in the first turn) | Commentary | implemented | last_shot_idx defaults to -100, so a unit that has not shot meets the 'previous turn' condition in the first turn (TerrainManager.gd:1024-1029). The stamp is written only when a unit shoots (ShootingPhase.gd:6079-6084). No other engine rule depends on the previous turn. |
| 13-16 | 13.11.01 FAQ ('objective marker' means 'objective') | FAQ | implemented | Codex rules that mention objective markers call MissionManager.model_in_objective_range, which covers terrain objectives (RulesEngine.gd:10061-10072; FactionAbilityManager.gd:1455-1469; SecondaryMissionManager.gd:2325-2337). |
| 13-16 | 13.11.01 Commentary (Gone to Ground) | Commentary | partial | Detection range drops 3″ when the model is hidden and a sight line is blocked by dense terrain, with a 9″ floor (TerrainManager.gd:1055-1072,1088,1121-1133). Two problems: Shadow Masters rolls back the shot stamp, so units that shot can still go to ground (SMDetachmentsA.gd:815-827); and dense terrain-area boundaries count as dense features (TerrainManager.gd:1182-1186). |
| 13-16 | 14.01 FAQ (within a terrain objective) | FAQ | implemented | A model is in range of a terrain objective when any part of its footprint overlaps a hosting area. The test is shape-aware and includes rectangular hulls (MissionManager.gd:511-516; Measurement.gd:414-434). With no elevation, models on upper floors count by footprint. |
| 13-16 | 14.01.01 Commentary (objective markers) | Commentary | implemented | An open-ground marker is in range when the base edge is within 3″ + 20 mm of its centre, i.e. 3″ of its closest part (MissionManager.gd:517-521, 793-848). Markers never block movement. The 5″ vertical limit cannot arise because models have no elevation. |
| 13-16 | 14.02 FAQ (one model's OC counts for several objectives) | FAQ | implemented | Each objective sums the OC of every model in range on its own (MissionManager.gd:753-856, 878-902). |
| 13-16 | 14.02.01 Commentary (control determined first at end of phase/turn) | Commentary | partial | Movement phase: control is determined after Rapid Ingress, then again after the end-of-phase windows (MovementPhase.gd:2165-2196, 5831-5833, 5853, 5874-5877). Charge phase: only after Heroic Intervention (ChargePhase.gd:1812-1864). End of turn: determined again after coherency removal, redeploys and hooks, just before scoring (ScoringPhase.gd:430-532; MissionManager.gd:132-134). |
| 13-16 | 14.03 FAQ (secure an objective taken at end of Movement phase) | FAQ | implemented | _complete_movement_phase determines control first, then runs the Thievin' Scavengers / Raise the Banner secure rules (MovementPhase.gd:5831-5842). |
| 13-16 | 14.03 FAQ (Insane Bravery on a battle-shocked unit) | FAQ | implemented | At 11e, 'insane_bravery' resolves to insane_bravery_11e (StratagemManager.gd:933-948,965). The old 10e exemption no longer matches, so battle-shocked targets are refused (:1073-1076). CommandPhase offers it only through can_use_stratagem (CommandPhase.gd:499-510). |
| 13-16 | 15.01 Errata (Grenades = Explosives) | Errata | implemented | At 11e, _resolve_core_id maps 'grenade' to 'explosives', which shares CP and usage tracking (StratagemManager.gd:927-944). Explosives accepts EXPLOSIVES or GRENADES units. |
| 13-16 | 15.01 Errata (Tank Shock = Crushing Impact) | Errata | implemented | _resolve_core_id maps 'tank_shock' to 'crushing_impact' (StratagemManager.gd:943-944). Codex discounts accept either id or name (SMAbilitiesA.gd:901; OrkAbilities11e.gd:1060-1065). |
| 13-16 | 15.01 Stratagem errata (Grenades) | Stratagem errata | implemented | Duplicate of the Grenades errata; same id mapping (StratagemManager.gd:941-942). |
| 13-16 | 15.01 Stratagem errata (Tank Shock) | Stratagem errata | implemented | Duplicate of the Tank Shock errata; same id mapping (StratagemManager.gd:943-944). |
| 13-16 | 15.01 FAQ (must still pay an increased cost) | FAQ | implemented | The only cost increase in the engine, Lord of Deceit, is added to the price at validation and at use (StratagemManager.gd:1013-1015, 1377-1383). |
| 13-16 | 15.01 FAQ (cannot pay an increased cost) | FAQ | no_engine_surface | No cost increase is applied after selection. Lord of Deceit is priced up front and can_use_stratagem refuses a use the player cannot afford (StratagemManager.gd:1013-1030), so a stratagem is never selected and then left unpaid. |
| 13-16 | 15.01 FAQ (optional +1CP section sets the original cost) | FAQ | wrong | Heroic Intervention 11e is a flat 1CP (StratagemManager.gd:5110-5119). Into the Fray's +1CP is never charged (ChargePhase.gd:3855-3876; HeroicInterventionDialog.gd:159-174). |
| 13-16 | 15.01.01 Commentary (modifying CP cost) | Commentary | implemented | Every discount is clamped at 0 and worked out per use (StratagemManager.gd:1005-1027, 1361-1409). |
| 13-16 | 15.01.02 Commentary (affected by a stratagem) | Commentary | no_engine_surface | A definition only. The 15.01 one-stratagem-per-unit and battle-shock checks already key on the target unit (StratagemManager.gd:1041-1076). No implemented rule uses the term. |
| 13-16 | 15.01.03 Commentary (your/opponent's stratagems, 'your unit') | Commentary | no_engine_surface | Definitions only; stratagem effects are applied to the selected target unit (StratagemManager.gd:1490). |
| 13-16 | 16.01 FAQ (continue an action after becoming battle-shocked) | FAQ | wrong | complete_actions cancels the action of every battle-shocked unit (ActionsManager.gd:272-276), even though Conservai and Tactical Mainstay units may start actions while battle-shocked (:137-146). RULINGS.md:16 records the opposite ruling. |
| 17-25 | 17.01 FAQ (M/V move through friendly units on non-normal/advance moves) | FAQ | implemented | No friendly-base check blocks an M/V on fall-back (only the enemy check, MovementPhase.gd:759, which is limited to NORMAL/ADVANCE), charge (ChargePhase.gd:2232-2330 checks endpoints only), pile-in or consolidate. Caveat: the 10e 'no model crosses any M/V base' check (MovementPhase.gd:766, 7477-7531) still blocks crossing a friendly M/V, which 11e leaves ambiguous. The related 17.01 launch-text omission is reported as its own gap. |
| 17-25 | 17.02 FAQ (models above ground floor / FRAME overhang are within the terrain area) | FAQ | implemented | 'Within a terrain area' is a 2D footprint test with no floor or elevation condition (TerrainManager.pieces_model_within_of, TerrainManager.gd:927-938; area_at, 847). Elevation only feeds the visibility rules (1128-1129). Vehicles are hull footprints, but the within-test approximates them as a base_mm circle. |
| 17-25 | 17.03 Errata ([BLAST] excluded vs engaged M/V) | Errata | partial | The player path blocks native [BLAST] at any engaged target (ShootingType.gd:47-51; CloseQuartersShooting target_allowed). The AI's atomic SHOOT only runs validate_shoot/validate_blast_targeting, which skips the shooter's own engagement (RulesEngine.gd:11308-11309). |
| 17-25 | 18.05 Errata (Emergency Disembark, filed under 17.03) | Errata | missing | _emergency_disembark_candidates rejects every spot within engagement range (RulesEngine.gd:17441), and a model with no spot is destroyed (17397). There is no 'set up engaged' fallback. |
| 17-25 | 17.03 FAQ (Heroic Intervention on a unit that rapid-disembarked) | FAQ | missing | Latent: 11e HI eligibility (StratagemManager.gd:4338-4423, ChargePhase.gd:3737-3801) never checks charge-ineligibility (flags.cannot_charge, set by rapid disembark at DisembarkMove.gd:124-125). It cannot come up today because no implemented rule (e.g. Pilin' Out) lets a unit rapid-disembark in the opponent's turn. |
| 17-25 | 17.03.01 Commentary (engaged with M/V still not eligible to shoot) | Commentary | implemented | Normal shooting refuses engaged units (NormalShooting.gd:16-17). Close-quarters shooting needs CQ weapons or an M/V shooter, and non-M/V models may only target units engaged with them (CloseQuartersShooting.gd). The AI path mirrors this in validate_shoot (RulesEngine.gd:6116, 6204). |
| 17-25 | 18.02 FAQ (abilities while embarked; Firing Deck example) | FAQ | implemented | Embarking nulls every model position (MovementPhase.gd:9184-9197, TransportManager.gd:224-227), so measurements and auras from embarked units fail (UnitAbilityManager.gd:3633-3655, 3562). Firing Deck loans fire as the TRANSPORT's own bearers (RulesEngine.gd:8702-8727). |
| 17-25 | 18.02 FAQ (rule allowing embark in the turn it disembarked overrides set-up ban) | FAQ | no_engine_surface | No implemented rule grants embarking in the turn a unit disembarked. The base ban is enforced (TransportManager.gd:155-166, MovementPhase.gd:9142-9144). |
| 17-25 | 18.02.01 Commentary (all embark conditions must be met) | Commentary | partial | Keywords, capacity and 'set up this turn' are checked in can_embark. The 3" test runs only when the prompt is offered (MovementPhase.gd:8349) and only over the bodyguard's own models (TransportManager.gd:784-794). _validate_embark_unit (9120-9151) never re-checks range. |
| 17-25 | 18.03 FAQ (any disembark move = disembarked this turn) | FAQ | implemented | Every disembark path sets a marker: emergency disembark sets disembarked_this_phase (RulesEngine.gd:17210), and the move templates set flags.disembarked_this_turn (DisembarkMove.gd:115, EmergencyDisembarkMove.gd:39). Consumers read both (SMDetachmentsA.gd:253-256), and both clear at end of turn (ScoringPhase.gd:941-953). |
| 17-25 | 18.04.01 Commentary (rapid disembark after ingress follows the transport's restrictions) | Commentary | missing | _validate_confirm_disembark (MovementPhase.gd:8478-8608) checks only set-up distance, engagement range (8558-8570), board edge, overlaps and the codex-only Drop Pod 8" rule (8603). The ingress restrictions are never applied to passengers. |
| 17-25 | 19.01 FAQ (Munitorum Field Manual attach list wins) | FAQ | no_engine_surface | Attach permissions are data: roster leader_data.can_lead with the Wahapedia Datasheets_leader.csv as fallback (LeaderPairingsLoader.gd:3-8). Which publication that data mirrors is a data question, not engine logic. |
| 17-25 | 19.01.01 Commentary (leaders remain a single unit after bodyguard destroyed) | Commentary | wrong | check_bodyguard_destroyed detaches every attached character into an independent unit once the bodyguard has no models (CharacterAttachmentManager.gd:275-299). This splits a leader+support pair and judges each on its own starting strength. |
| 17-25 | 19.03 FAQ (destroyed models don't contribute keywords) | FAQ | wrong | attached_unit_keywords unions every attached character's keywords with no alive check (CharacterAttachmentManager.gd:158-176), and [ANTI] uses that union (RulesEngine.gd:11006-11028). The codex group_has_keyword helpers do the same, and the data has no per-model keywords. |
| 17-25 | 19.04 FAQ ('this unit' rules apply to all; single-model rules e.g. FNP/LO only to that model) | FAQ | partial | 'This unit' effects reach the whole attached unit. However, 11e allocation folds characters into a copy of the bodyguard (RulesEngine.gd:4367-4393) and takes FNP from that copy (4137, 3744-3748, get_model_fnp 16808-16825). A leader's own 'Feel No Pain X+' is therefore never rolled for him while attached. |
| 17-25 | 19.04.01 Commentary (benefits end when the conferring models die) | Commentary | partial | The Orks 11e module filters dead components (OrkAbilities11e.gd:258-267). The Custodes, Space Marines and Space Wolves group helpers include dead attached characters (CustodesAbilities.gd:193-207, CustodesEnhancements.gd:238-262, SMAbilitiesA.gd:308-323, SMDetachmentsA.gd:218-229/323-329, SWAbilities11e.gd:239-255). The legacy UnitAbilityManager while_leading flags last until the end of the phase. |
| 17-25 | 20.01 FAQ (placing units in reserves during the battle may exceed 50%) | FAQ | implemented | The 50% cap is only checked in FormationsPhase (425-455) and DeploymentPhase (482-502); repositioning during the battle is uncapped. Side note: the engine also enforces a 10e Chapter Approved 50% unit-count cap and measures against total army points rather than the battle-size points limit. Neither comes from 11e 20.01. |
| 17-25 | 20.01 FAQ (cap only applies until start of battle round 1) | FAQ | implemented | The only callers of the cap are pre-battle (FormationsPhase.gd:438-455, DeploymentPhase.gd:487-502). Nothing re-checks it after deployment. |
| 17-25 | 20.01.01 Commentary ('Reserves' = strategic reserves) | Commentary | implemented | Deep Strike and strategic reserves share UnitStatus.IN_RESERVES. The caps, arrival and the end-of-round-3 sweep treat both as reserves (GameState.gd:1024-1031, ScoringPhase.gd:955-1000). |
| 17-25 | 20.01.02 Commentary (SR units destroyed at end of final turn, no on-destroyed triggers) | Commentary | missing | No end-of-battle step destroys units left in strategic reserves; only the end-of-round-3 sweep exists (ScoringPhase.gd:584-587). The commentary says this destruction triggers nothing, and no engine scoring path was found that would differ, so no gap is filed. |
| 17-25 | 20.03.01 Commentary ('set up as Reinforcements' = made an ingress move) | Commentary | implemented | Both PLACE_REINFORCEMENT and Rapid Ingress apply IngressMove.after_moving_effects (MovementPhase.gd:4725, 2614; IngressMove.gd:138-146). |
| 17-25 | 21.03 FAQ (take to the skies applies when selected to move; declare before advance/charge roll) | FAQ | partial | Advance records the declaration before the roll (MovementPhase.gd:1272-1277), but SET_TAKE_TO_SKIES still accepts an ADVANCE after the roll (7192-7230). Charges have no take to the skies at all (no 'skies' anywhere in ChargePhase.gd or ChargeMove11e.gd). Normal/advance/fall-back moves give FLY units the crossing and terrain benefits without any declaration (759/766/7361). |
| 17-25 | 22.03.01 Commentary (same psychic ability once per phase) | Commentary | implemented | The Librarius budget records each psychic ability per phase and refuses a repeat (SWAbilities11e.gd:430-433, 448-466). SMAbilitiesA/B share the same keys (SMAbilitiesA.gd:193-197, SMAbilitiesB.gd:186-189), and Ork Unstable Energies does the same (RULINGS.md:116). |
| 17-25 | 23.04 FAQ (Firing Deck: weapon buffs from the other unit don't carry over) | FAQ | implemented | Loaned weapons become synthetic bearers on the transport hull, with the transport as the attacker (RulesEngine.gd:8702-8727). Grants keyed on the embarked unit therefore don't follow the weapon, e.g. Supa-snazz Dakka keys on actor_unit (OrkEnhancements11e.gd:806-814). |
| 17-25 | 23.04 FAQ (meaning of X in Lone Operative X") | FAQ | implemented | get_lone_operative_range parses X from 'Lone Operative X"' and defaults to 12" (RulesEngine.gd:9958-9974). Every LO targeting gate uses it. |
| 17-25 | 23.04 FAQ ([HAZARDOUS] mortal wounds allocated to the unit per 06.02) | FAQ | wrong | resolve_hazardous_check puts all hazard mortal wounds on one model carrying the hazardous weapon (priority list at RulesEngine.gd:11672-11712, applied at 11545). Excess is discarded with no spill-over (11573). |
| 17-25 | 23.04 FAQ (Scouts / pre-battle rules alternate, first-turn player first) | FAQ | partial | The step starts with the first-turn player (FirstTurnRollOffPhase.gd:150-151, ScoutPhase.gd:38). It then resolves all of that player's Scout units before switching (ScoutPhase.gd:627-647), instead of alternating unit by unit. |
| 17-25 | 23.04 FAQ (Feel No Pain rolled in Resolve Damage of Inflict Damage) | FAQ | implemented | FNP is rolled per allocated attack after damage modifiers, inside the allocation/inflict-damage step (RulesEngine.gd:3744-3760; devastating wounds at 3832-3843). |
| 17-25 | 23.04 FAQ (Fight on Death resolved before Deadly Demise) | FAQ | implemented | Fight On Death keeps destroyed models on the board (alive=true) until the unit has fought or the phase ends (FightPhase.gd:3039-3054, 3177-3206). The removal diffs then run _check_kill_diffs, which triggers Deadly Demise (443-445). Side note: DD is rolled only when the whole unit dies, not per model as the 24.08 launch text says. |
| 17-25 | 23.04 FAQ ([ANTI-NON-MONSTER/VEHICLE] triggers vs units without the keyword) | FAQ | implemented | The negated form is parsed (RulesEngine.gd:9804-9813) and only crits against targets with none of the keywords (11017-11028). This applies in the ranged, auto-resolve and melee paths (1800, 3189, 5290, 14720). |
| 17-25 | 23.04 FAQ (vertical movement while taking to the skies doesn't count for [HEAVY]) | FAQ | implemented | FLY movers never accrue vertical or difficult-ground cost (MovementPhase.gd:7361-7362), so moved_max_inches (3451-3459), which heavy_applies_11e reads (ModifierStack.gd:168-187), is horizontal only. This is over-broad: it also covers FLY units that did not take to the skies (part of the 21.03 gap). |
| 17-25 | 24.01.01 Commentary (numbers after an ability are irrelevant when selecting by ability) | Commentary | implemented | Numbered abilities are matched by prefix: Deadly Demise (UnitAbilityManager.gd:4199-4214), Scouts (GameState.gd:708, 745), 'Feel No Pain X+' (RulesEngine.get_named_fnp) and Lone Operative (contains match, RulesEngine.gd:9834-9846). |
| 17-25 | 24.11.01 Commentary (EXTRA ATTACKS weapons usable without a non-EA weapon) | Commentary | implemented | [EXTRA ATTACKS] weapons are exempt from the one-weapon rule (FightPhase.gd:1235-1237), and an EA-only assignment is a valid non-empty assignment (893-904). FightController lists every melee weapon (1001-1030). |
| 17-25 | 24.24.01 Commentary (Lone Operative in attached units) | Commentary | partial | Latent: LO is never applied to a target that has attached characters (RulesEngine.gd:7203-7206, 6188-6191, 1636-1638), so the all-LO attached unit and highest-X cases are not modelled. 'LO active again' only works because the engine detaches characters (CharacterAttachmentManager.gd:275-299). No current datasheet gives a bodyguard LO. |
| 17-25 | 24.26.01 Commentary (each identical [ONE SHOT] weapon fires once) | Commentary | partial | Latent: weapon ids are de-duplicated per model (RulesEngine.gd:7724-7726), and one-shot use is tracked per (model, weapon id) (9096-9130). A second identical one-shot weapon could never fire, but no current roster carries two. |
| 17-25 | 24.28.01 Commentary (PRECISION + DEVASTATING WOUNDS mortal wounds go to the chosen CHARACTER group) | Commentary | missing | Precision only reorders the save allocation (RulesEngine.gd:3661-3669). Devastating-wound mortal wounds go through Allocation.apply_devastating_wounds_11e (3836; Allocation.gd:376-399), which uses the 06.02 order (non-CHARACTER first) and ignores the precision group. |
| 17-25 | 24.37.01 Commentary ([TORRENT] cannot have [INDIRECT FIRE] or [PRECISION]) | Commentary | partial | Latent: granted [PRECISION] has no TORRENT guard (RulesEngine.gd:3508, 5675; Space Wolves Headhunters at SWAbilities11e.gd:603-609). Only the AI estimator drops INDIRECT for torrent weapons (17938). No current weapon or grant combination triggers it. |
| 17-25 | 25.04 FAQ (an attached unit can be given an Upgrade) | FAQ | implemented | Upgrades may go on any non-EPIC HERO unit, bodyguards included (EnhancementRules11e.gd:11-15, bearer_problem). |
| 17-25 | 25.04 FAQ (a unit cannot have both an Upgrade and an enhancement) | FAQ | implemented | validate_army counts Upgrades and enhancements together per unit and flags more than one (EnhancementRules11e.gd:309-335). This is an advisory warning surfaced by ArmyListManager.gd:1828-1833, not a block. |
| 17-25 | 25.04 FAQ (Upgrades are enhancements) | FAQ | implemented | Upgrades are enhancement records (upgrade_tag, EnhancementRules11e.gd:236-237) and count as one choice toward the 2-per-1000-points cap (338-356). |
